OK so both cameras say the plate center ≈ (0.06,0.197).

Actually (0.185 vs 0.197): 1.2cm in -y, and x 0.055 vs 0.0575: 2.5mm. So the bowl is nearly perfectly centered over the plate already.

Hmm, but wait: is the bowl's apparent center in the agentview reliable? The bowl is hanging with the gripper gripping its rim, and hanging bowls tilt. From the agentview image at step 7, the bowl looks slightly tilted but upright-ish. The wrist view shows a big bowl below-right of the gripper — the bowl's center is offset from the camera axis because the camera looks along the eef axis and the bowl hangs -y. Since the eef is at y=0.229, and plate center is 0.197, the bowl at y≈0.19 means the bowl hangs -0.04 in y from the eef. ✓ consistent with earlier -0.05 estimate.

So current alignment: bowl center (0.055,0.185) vs plate center (0.058,0.197). Off by 1.2cm in y — fine (bowl radius 5cm).

Now descend. Bowl bottom ≈ eef_z - 0.064 = 1.319-0.064 = 1.255. Plate top at 0.908-0.915. So descend 0.34 in z. That's a lot but z-only, fine, with step_clip 0.01, max_steps 120 (0.34/0.01=34 steps minimum... OSC action_scale... steps needed maybe 60).
